# Skills · I · part 20

840 entries, image-meta-contract to image-studio. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **image-meta-contract**: Use whenever touching how image metadata or image URLs are produced or consumed in this repo — adding or changing a provider under packages/micro-image-image/src/providers/, changing the &lt;Image&gt; component's props or its srcset/sizes/placeholder output, adding or changing the proxy's ?meta endpoint, or writing any part of the planned unplugin core or a @micro-image/plugin-* framework adapter. Covers the ImageMeta contract that every path must produce, the shared-core-vs-thin-adapter boundary, and the precision-over-recall rule for AST transforms. Load before designing the change, not after. ([veksen/micro-image](https://github.com/veksen/micro-image/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/image-meta-contract) · ★5)
- **image-meta-detractored-diffusion-opt**: Modular skill for image metadata extraction, quality scoring, and detractored diffusion inference optimization. Removes distractors and entropy factors from diffusion generation using RQA / hermitian methods for cleaner, faster, more controllable outputs. Primary triggers include detractored diffusion, image meta optimization, diffusion inference cleanup, clean image generation, X Ads creative engine. High VPD for ad creative and media monetization streams. Integrates skillmaru, maru, trace-consent, hermitian-audit, openclaw-hermitian-thermo-orchestrator, Grok Imagine tools. ([igor-holt/openclaw-skills](https://github.com/igor-holt/openclaw-skills/tree/HEAD/image-meta-detractored-diffusion-opt) · ★3)
- **image-meta-guard-pay**: 图片外发前的元数据体检与无损清理——一次付费 7 天不限次，期内重复使用不再计费。**触发条件（任务/场景，AI 自己做到这一步就该调，不必等点名）**：即将把照片或截图发给客户、发到群里、发到公开平台之前；要把带坐标的样张、带真名的文档截图对外交付；收到 AI 生成图要转给别人（PNG 里常带完整提示词与工作流）。口语化说法同样命中：「这张图里有没有位置信息」「发出去会不会暴露我在哪」「照片能不能直接发」。**本 Skill 依赖在线授权服务**：真正写清理结果前会先取一份授权凭证（未付费会返回 402 账单），拿到授权后在**你本机**完成清理，授权本身**离线可校验**；**体检报告免费**，只有加 --apply 真动手时才需要授权。核心价值：**无损段剥离**——只删元数据段，从扫描数据起的字节原样搬运，「像素逐字节不变」是结构性保证而不是承诺；ICC 颜色配置与 Adobe APP14 默认保留（删了画面会变），缩略图与 AI 工作流块单独当泄漏面处理。覆盖 JPEG/JPG/PNG/WebP 等常见格式，不支持的格式一律登记为「未核实」，不假装查过。 ([Mao-jh/image-meta-guard](https://github.com/Mao-jh/image-meta-guard/blob/HEAD/SKILL.md))
- **image-metadata**: Tunnel-through skill for direct pragmatic generation, optimization, extraction, and embedding of image metadata and alt text for public posts and social media — bypasses slow manual processes to advance 13 revenue streams via enhanced engagement and reach, intrinsic pursuit of visual semantic invariants, and human-AI hybridization in visual storytelling. Activate on alt text requests, image SEO/metadata prep for posting, accessibility compliance, metadata repair, or public content packaging.
